# RTG STATE ATLAS v0.1 (built 2026-10-21)
AI-only reader. Derived and NONCANONICAL: never the source of a claim; on any conflict the owner DSN wins. Atlas owner (Claude) advises, stream owners decide, PI (Mustafa) holds final authority. Method: claude/atlas/RTG_STATE_ATLAS_METHOD_v0_1.md. Current state only: no history, no review verdicts, no proofs (one-line method pointer at most). Body is grouped by subject domain D1–D6, never by stream; stream names appear only as provenance (@OWNER§).

## L0 KEY
record: `ID GRADE | statement | sc: scope | if: premises | <dep | >used-by | ¬ not-claimed | cert | @OWNER§`
ID = owner RID; `*STREAM:slug` = no owner RID (itself a D-RID item). Local non-RID keys (ER2B-Cxx, RER-*, FMS session labels) are navigation keys, not RIDs.
edges: `<` depends on · `>` consumed by · `≡` same object/alias · `⊥` symbol collision · `!` tension/conflict · `~` atlas-derived link (verify before use)
grade qualifiers: `·c` conditional · `·s` structural · `·dx` diagnostic-only · `nc` node from a noncanonical source
global scope rules (always on): SG-PROG-1 no physical continuous↔discrete transfer without a named proved bridge · SG-PROG-2 results conditional on the selected generator T are not physically inevitable · SG-PROG-3 no derived smooth coupling before metric + dictionary · SG-PROG-4 document location creates no transfer authority (full text D1.L4)
L5 classes: D-RID · D-PROOF · D-GRADE · D-CONV · D-SYM · D-PIN · D-STALE · D-SCOPE · D-LINK · D-WRONG · PASS (atlas re-did the arithmetic). L5 IDs = STREAM.<slice id> so each maps to one Atlas Advisory Note row.

### L0.M Grade semantics (DSN_METHODOLOGY v1.3 §§3, 4, 8, 9; kinds of assertion, not one ladder)
DEF definition/adopted axiom (adoption ≠ physical necessity) · THM complete proof under explicit assumptions (convention independence, universality are extra) · THM-ALG via IA / ALG via IA: archived rigorous interval certificate over the full stated domain · ALG exact derivation in declared field/domain/conventions (finite exact checks = finite scope) · CAND = ALG candidate (exact-form conjecture) · ALG·s = ALG structure (proved part separated) · ENG validated infrastructure (not physics proof) · NUM finite-scope protocol · NUM strong = NUM + named robustness checks (precision alone insufficient) · OBS uncharacterized · HEUR / HEUR strong interpretation (never theorem-grade) · HYP unproved working hypothesis · OPEN workflow · FAIL rejected claim/route at exact scope (record permanent; decision correctable).
Workflow: RES = RESULT, always [from: TAG], weakest undischarged premise governs · COR correction · RET withdrawal (≠ proof of falsity) · SUPERSEDED (≠ refutation) · ARCHIVE-READY (snapshot only).
Qualifiers (one registry): conv, family, scope, horizon, assuming, bridge-required, obs-fit, import (exact version + RID), proof, provenance (source-read / source-reported / artifact-inspected / independently-checked), RID (identity, not status). Local aliases cannot override canonical tags; mixtures [ALG/NUM], [ENG/ALG] must be unpacked (atlas flags D-GRADE).
Promotion: NUM→NUM strong named checks · NUM/HEUR strong→CAND explicit conjecture · CAND→ALG exact derivation (floating precision / integer-relation guessing never qualify) · ALG→THM complete proof at claimed quantifiers · finite horizon→all-parameter needs general argument · convention-fixed→invariant needs invariance proof; no automatic ALG→THM. Demotion: proof gap ⇒ keep proved part, rest CAND/ALG·s/OPEN; narrower domain ⇒ correct overclaim; failed independent check ⇒ NUM; missing artifact ⇒ provenance limitation, not refutation.
RIDs: category word (THM, ALG, DEF, NUM, OBS, HYP, CAND, RESULT, OPEN, ART, ENG, FAIL) is immutable identity text, not live status; only the owner registers/reassigns; namespace registry METH §4.2 (UVM = OPEN M; OFN shortname ≠ alias of RTG-OFN). Missing owner RID: never invent or borrow an OPEN-module RID; write "[import: SRC vX.Y §… | RID unavailable]" + governance request (atlas D-RID). Import = exact version (never "current"), RID primary, Source status, Depends on, Verification, Synchronization; reference-only summary ≠ load-bearing import ≠ locally proved consequence (may get local RID, never re-owns). A newer source version does not invalidate an older pin automatically (atlas: D-PIN only when the claim changed or RID moved; pin drift alone = D-STALE). Cross-branch physical use needs a named ALG/THM bridge (= SG-PROG-1).

PROG-TIERS DEF | navigation only: 0 programme · 1 methodology · 2 foundational/historical/common-home (OFN, LEGACY, PREL, NUMGEN) · 3 continuous (DYAD, TRIAD, GEN, UVM, RECOG, DZT) · 4 discrete (TDRG) · 5 shared infra (AZ); 14 document homes ≠ 14 branches; GEN header "Tier 3/4" placed Tier 3, discrete inputs = blocked interfaces @PROG§6.1
